- **gitnexus-index**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/bash/gitnexus-index) · ★745)
- **gitnexus-index**: 管理 GitNexus 代码索引：创建（首次 analyze）、增量更新（status 检测过期后自动更新）、强制重建与修复（--force / --repair-fts / --embeddings）。触发："创建代码索引"、"更新代码索引"、"索引过期了"、"重新建索引"、"修复索引"、"gitnexus 索引"。 ([HACK-WU/skills](https://github.com/HACK-WU/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gitnexus-index) · ★3)
- **gitnexus-knowledge-graph**: MCP-native knowledge graph engine for AI coding agents. Indexes entire repos into a structured graph (functions, classes, imports, call chains, execution flows) using Tree-sitter ASTs and exposes it via 7 MCP tools. Gives Claude Code, Cursor, and other agents full codebase structural awareness so they stop missing dependencies and breaking call chains. Use when vibe coding large projects, refactoring, understanding unfamiliar codebases, or before making changes that could have cascading effects. ([spoonyc/claude-science-skills](https://github.com/spoonyc/claude-science-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gitnexus-knowledge-graph))
- **gitnexus-lfg**: Use when the user wants the GitNexus engineering pipeline run end-to-end on a task: gitnexus-plan (plan depth chosen up front), a blocking gate to execute with gitnexus-work or stop, finishing with a gitnexus-review of the result. Examples: "/gitnexus-lfg Add retry support to the ingestion pipeline", "run the gitnexus pipeline on this", "plan, build and review this feature". ([abhigyanpatwari/GitNexus](https://github.com/abhigyanpatwari/GitNexus/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/gitnexus-lfg) · 28 copies)
- **gitnexus-list**: List all GitNexus-indexed repositories. Triggers: "list repos" | "gitnexus list" | "what repos are indexed" | "show indexed repos". ([Roxabi/roxabi-plugins](https://github.com/Roxabi/roxabi-plugins/tree/HEAD/archived/plugins/gitnexus/skills/list) · ★10)
- **gitnexus-no-churn**: Set up or repair GitNexus in a repository so it never causes churn — no "index is N commits behind" after every commit and no CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md rewrites from `gitnexus analyze`. Use when adding GitNexus to a repo, when the index reports itself stale/behind, when `gitnexus analyze` dirties CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md/.claude/skills, or when asked to "apply the gitnexus no-churn pattern". Works with husky, lefthook, pre-commit or plain git hooks, any language. ([nwlnexus/agent-plugins](https://github.com/nwlnexus/agent-plugins/tree/HEAD/skills/gitnexus-no-churn))
- **gitnexus-obsidian-github**: Unified workflow linking GitHub repos, GitNexus knowledge graphs, and Obsidian vault notes for seamless project intelligence ([UltronCore/claude-skill-vault](https://github.com/UltronCore/claude-skill-vault/tree/HEAD/skills/productivity/gitnexus-obsidian-github) · ★1)
- **gitnexus-pdg-query**: Use when querying or extending GitNexus's PDG control/data-dependence surface (the `pdg_query` MCP tool, CDG/REACHING_DEF edges), or reasoning about "what controls X" / "where does Y flow" / guard clauses. Examples: "what guards this statement?", "trace this variable within the function", "why is the pdg_query result empty?", "add a CDG query". ([ronsilver/agent-covenant](https://github.com/ronsilver/agent-covenant/tree/HEAD/content/skills/gitnexus-pdg-query) · ★1 · 29 copies)
- **gitnexus-plan**: Use when you need a deep, implementation-ready engineering plan for a code change — built from GitNexus graph intelligence, statement-level PDG analysis, and targeted source verification, compact enough that an implementation agent can start without re-investigating. Also strengthens existing plans via Deepen mode. Examples: "/gitnexus-plan Add retry support to the ingestion pipeline", "/gitnexus-plan deepen docs/plans/&lt;plan&gt;.md", "plan this change using the knowledge graph". ([abhigyanpatwari/GitNexus](https://github.com/abhigyanpatwari/GitNexus/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/gitnexus-plan) · 28 copies)
- **gitnexus-policy**: 判斷在 Buddha-skills 或任何裝有 gitnexus 的專案中，何時該用 `gitnexus analyze`（輕量增量），何時該用 `gitnexus analyze --embeddings --skills --verbose`（完整重建）。當使用者問「要不要重跑索引」「需要 embeddings 嗎」「為什麼這次要全開」「gitnexus 怎麼跑」「gitnexus 哪些 flag 該加」「indexer 跑出來怪怪的」時使用。也會在 `auto-iterate`、`maintenance-patrol`、`new-project-init`、寫 ARCHITECTURE 文件、跨 repo 語意搜尋、debug 索引異常時被引用。 ([LostSunset/Buddha-skills](https://github.com/LostSunset/Buddha-skills/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/gitnexus-policy))

- **gitnexus-repo-map**: Use GitNexus as a lightweight repo-exploration tool to read unfamiliar codebases, generate architecture/module/dependency maps, and inspect functional clusters. Treat GitNexus as a hand-held knife, NOT a harness subsystem — never run `gitnexus setup` automatically, never register Claude Code hooks, never modify global `~/.claude/`, and never promote `.claude/skills/generated/` into the canonical manifest. CLI-first; web UI is opt-in. Trigger phrases include: "GitNexus / repo map / visual code graph / architecture topology / read this open-source repo / 仓库架构 / 代码拓扑 / 模块地图 / clusters / 读开源项目".
